## 2026-11-03 - I-frame trick-play for on-demand HLS (build queue 3.2b, first half; v0.31.0)

What: optional I-frame playlists for the players that scrub with them (AVPlayer on Apple devices and Safari, smart TVs). Off by default; a switch per output profile and an account default for new profiles.

- **Switch** (migration `0123_trick_play.sql`, `modules/outputProfiles.ts`, `modules/accountSettings.ts`): `PATCH /api/output-profiles/{id}` `{trickPlay}` (administrators, audited); new profiles start with `accounts.trick_play_default`. Console: Account settings > Trick-play for Apple and smart-TV players.
- **Which switch an asset follows** (`services/trickplay/store.ts`): clip or recording, its group's profile; upload, the profile its ladder mirrors; else the account default. Cached a minute per asset.
- **Playlists** (`services/trickplay/iframes.ts`, pure; `vod_iframe_playlists`; job `trickplay.build`): byte ranges from each fragment's moof to the end of its opening keyframe, keys and maps carried. Made after every build when on, and on the first master served with the switch on: existing media gets them with no re-encode. Listed in masters and served at `<rendition>/iframes.m3u8` (server.ts) only while on; off keeps them unlisted; a re-encode's old ones are not served.
- **Web SDK** (`volt-player/src/sdk/engine.ts`): Shaka `manifest.disableIFrames`, so web viewers never load them.
- **Docs**: Account settings guide (section, screenshot, API rows), API reference overlay, routes and OpenAPI regenerated.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean. `trickplay.check.ts` 53/53 (byte ranges against the segments and a ground-truth decode, off by default, switched on later, keys, which switch, permissions); browser 10/10 on a fresh docs seed; 14 neighbour suites green (vod-ads needs `deploy/terraform`, not in the container copy).
- **Not done**: 3.2b-2 (DASH trick-mode, live and FAST through the rights variant service, iOS SDK, Safari check on the Mac). A keyframe that does not open its fragment is not listed (a range always starts at the moof); Volt's segmenters open each fragment on a keyframe, so this only thins entries where a GOP is shorter than a fragment.

- **Docs**: RELAY.md and DECISION_mp3_delivery.md superseded with the decision, RADIO.md, engineering guides, APPLY.md, customer guides (Radio, radio now-playing, overview, how Volt fits together, home, live streams, analytics, play gate); Radio screenshot retaken.

## 2026-11-03 - DASH trick-mode for on-demand assets (build queue 3.2b-2; v0.34.0)

What: the DASH half of optional trick-play. With an asset's switch on, its MPD gains a trick-mode AdaptationSet per video set (`EssentialProperty` `http://dashif.org/guidelines/trickmode`) for smart-TV and other DASH players. Live and FAST are split to 3.2b-3.

- **Trick segments** (`services/trickplay/iframes.ts` `keyframeFragment`, pure): the HLS I-frame byte range (moof, mdat header, keyframe) re-wrapped as a fragment of its own: a new moof listing only the keyframe, timed to the next one, and an mdat of exactly its bytes. Made per request from a ranged read (`store.ts` `readClipsRange`: disk, else an S3 range), nothing stored or re-encoded. CENC renditions keep the keyframe's senc entry with saiz and saio pointing at it and the moof's pssh; a track with sample groups (sbgp) gets no DASH set (its HLS I-frames are unaffected).
- **MPD** (`withDashTrickMode`): after each video AdaptationSet with a built rendition, matched by its init segment (`$RepresentationID$` and `$Bandwidth$` resolved, so Shaka Packager and ffmpeg MPDs both work); codecs, size, `maxPlayoutRate` (keyframe interval in pictures), `codingDependency="false"`, the main set's ContentProtection carried. Left alone with more than one Period (stitched ads), a BaseURL, or trick sets already there.
- **Store and route**: `vod_iframe_playlists.dash` (migration `0125_trick_play_dash.sql`) holds each keyframe's source range and time; a row from before (NULL) is rebuilt on the next MPD. `trick/<key>/<n>.m4s` beside the MPD (server.ts), 404 when off, older build or past the end.
- **Fixed** (found on a real encode): Shaka Packager writes media playlists beside the master (`stream_0.m3u8`), so 3.2b gave every rendition the same `iframes.m3u8`; I-frame playlists are now `<media playlist>.iframes.m3u8`. A FAST ladder's master and MPD under the asset (`ladders/...`) no longer get the asset's I-frame lines.
- **iOS SDK**: AVPlayer uses the I-frame playlists itself; noted in PLAYER_SDK_IOS.md (project doc), not tried on a device.
- **Web SDK**: its `disableIFrames` already turns off DASH trick-mode too (Shaka's one setting); comment updated.
- **Docs**: Account settings guide (DASH, encrypted media, iOS), API reference overlay, OpenAPI regenerated, change notes.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web, volt-player). New `trickplay-dash.check.ts` 66/66: a trick segment is one sample, an mdat of exactly the keyframe, its decode time, init plus it decodes to source frame 100; a whole trick track decodes to 10 pictures, the tenth frame 450; served ones decode to the right frame; CENC (AES-CTR, subsamples) carries senc/saiz/saio and decrypts with the key to frame 100, not with another; MPD set placement, ids, timeline 10 entries over 20 s, audio untouched, multi-Period untouched; off, on later, rebuild of old rows, off again, re-encode, ladder MPD and master; Shaka layout gives distinct I-frame playlists. New `trickplay-dash-browser.check.ts` 9/9: Shaka 4.16 in Chromium attaches a trick track to both renditions, 8x fast-forward fetches 12 trick segments (all 200) and moves 1.5 s to 25.1 s with no player or media error; with the SDK's setting none attached or fetched (VP9, since the container's Chromium has no H.264). trickplay 54/54 (URI change, one new), frames 81/81, smart-render-frames 62/62, vod-markers 40/40, bucket-tracks 28/28, companion-files 64/64, clips-store 28/28, vod-ads-catchup 47/47, vod-ads-vmap 56/56, jobs 43/43, geo-access 15/15. Not run here: vod-ads-sgai's browser part (volt-player node_modules not copied), vod-library's encoder probe (volt-encoder not copied), vod-library-pure 169/170 (the on-demand content type list, unrelated). Docs scrub and coverage clean.

## 2026-11-03 - DASH trick-mode on live channels and events (build queue 3.2b-4 (a); v0.36.0)

What: live channels and events with trick-play on, packaging DASH, get a trick-mode track in their MPD from their next start. FAST, playout-spliced streams, trick-play inside rights variants and Christian's Mac checks are split to 3.2b-5.

- **Why the packager's way** (the item asked to pick by cost): live MPDs and segments reach viewers from the origin or CloudFront without the API, so re-wrapping keyframes per request (the on-demand way) would put the API in front of every live trick request. The packager's `trick_play_factor` writes keyframe-only segments once, cached at the edge like any segment, and only for ONE small rendition: the smallest rung at 360p or above (else the smallest; package-only: the lowest bitrate input, else the last). A player shows nothing else while fast-forwarding, so the extra bytes are that rendition's keyframes, a few percent of the ladder.
- **Control plane** (`services/jobConfig.ts`): `trick_play_dash: true` when the profile's switch is on and DASH is delivered (`hls` profiles get nothing, `dash`-only profiles get only this). Its own key, so an older encoder image ignores it.
- **Encoder** (`volt-encoder/bin/encoder.py` `trick_rendition`, `build_shaka`): one more stream descriptor, `trick_play_factor=1,dash_only=1`, into `<label>_trick/` (label reduced to safe characters, `_safe_label`, shared with the I-frame playlist names). `dash_only` keeps it out of the HLS master, which has its own I-frame playlists.
- **Delivered MPD**: without playout, `manifest_cc.mpd` is the packager's MPD and keeps the set. With playout splicing, `manifest_cc.mpd` is built from the HLS media playlists (`playout.render_mpd`), and the trick stream has none, so it is never listed under a break or slate (3.2b-5 (c) makes it follow the splice).
- **Rights variants** (`services/variantManifest.ts` `renderVariantMpd`): every AdaptationSet with the dashif trickmode property is dropped first. Before, the loop Periods would have looked for a loop rendition matching `<label>_trick` and refused the whole variant (a blacked-out DASH audience would have lost the stream, not just trick-play).
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). `dev/test_trick_play.py` 29/29 (was 12; on HEAD 4 fail and then it errors), including a real playout splice driven over a packager MPD with a trick set. `trickplay-live` 12/12 (4 new). `edge-variant-manifests` 72/72 (6 new; 5 fail on HEAD). Neighbours in the container: edge-variants 62/62, edge-rights 38/38, rights-preview 50/50, trickplay 54/54, trickplay-dash 66/66, live-scte35 43/43, channel-admin 43/43, geo-access 15/15, stream-load 31/31, captions-plan 19/19, orchestrator ok. Encoder suites on the device: playout 64/64, companion-pdt 38/38, timed-events 65/65, fast-playout 31/31, id3-emsg 51/51, fairplay-drm 16/16, ladder-args 14/14, ingest-args 17/17, output-check 23/23, stream-metrics 10/10, source-selector, events and packager-log pass. Docs scrub and coverage clean; Account settings guide updated (no screenshot change: the console text was already true).
- **Not run here**: Shaka Packager is in neither the container nor the device shell, so its live trick-mode output (and that `trick_play_factor` with `dash_only` on a UDP input behaves as documented) has not been seen. Christian to check on the Mac, 3.2b-5 (e).
- **Not done**: the storage projection (2.4a) does not count the trick rendition's keyframes yet.

## 2026-11-03 - I-frame trick-play on FAST channels (build queue 3.2b-5; v0.37.0)

What: FAST channels with trick-play on (the group's output profile) get I-frame playlists in their HLS from their next start. Finished from the 20:24Z run's handoff after its link to the Mac dropped.

- **Control plane** (`services/fast/store.ts` `timelineFor`): `trickPlay` from the channel group's output profile. `modules/fast.ts`: a per-viewer session master (stitched ads) drops `#EXT-X-I-FRAME-STREAM-INF`, since the channel's lists index the house fill.
- **Playout** (`volt-encoder/bin/fast_playout.py`): `keyframe_range` (moof to the end of the opening keyframe; a non-sync first sample refused), `KeyframeCache` (16 KB head per segment from disk, S3 ranged GetObject or HTTP Range, bounded, retried), `Playout.iframes` (one entry per listed segment, same media and discontinuity sequence, init again at each join, an unknown range starts the list after it), `master_with_iframes` (once every rung has one; video codecs, RESOLUTION, peak keyframe BANDWIDTH). Breaks, slates and loops are covered as they air. Nothing encoded or stored.
- **Docs**: FAST guide (Put it on air), Account settings guide and console card, screenshot retaken.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device. `test_fast_trick_play.py` 37/37 on the device (every range decodes alone to exactly one keyframe, the segment's first picture), test_fast_playout 31/31; fast-beacons 52/52 (new session-master case fails on HEAD), fast-access 50/50, fast-ad-schedule 34/34, fast-ads 48/48, fast-brand 28/28, fast-graphics 15/15, fast-ladder 54/54, fast-lineup 62/62, trickplay-live 12/12, edge-variant-manifests 72/72, edge-variants 62/62. fast.check.sh and docs-seed-fast fail identically on HEAD in the container (no packager to encode the seed).

## 2026-11-03 - DASH trick-mode on FAST channels (build queue 3.2b-6 (a); v0.38.0)

What: FAST channels with trick-play on list a DASH trick-mode track. Spliced live streams, rights variants and Christian's checks are split to 3.2b-7.

- **Trick tracks per packaged asset** (`volt-app/src/services/trickplay/ladder.ts`, job `trickplay.ladder` in `jobs/handlers/trickPlay.ts`): one rendition (the smallest at 360p or above) cut into `trick/<rep>/seg_<n>.m4s` beside the asset's or ladder's MPD, each segment's opening keyframe re-wrapped as a fragment lasting the segment; `trick.json` written last. Local disk, and the origin bucket when the library is there. Made once, shared by every channel and airing.
- **Queued** by `services/fast/store.ts` `timelineFor` for every aired asset of a channel with trick-play on (once an hour per asset per task).
- **Playout** (`volt-encoder/bin/fast_playout.py`): `load_trick` reads trick.json (again each minute for assets without one); `dash(trick=)` adds AdaptationSet id 2 with the dashif trickmode property naming the video set (id 0) in every Period, same SegmentTimeline and numbers as the rendition. Fixed: `$RepresentationID$`/`$Bandwidth$` from an asset MPD resolved per asset.
- **Docs**: FAST and Account settings guides.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean. trickplay-fast-dash 28/28 (ground-truth decodes, playout MPD, Shaka in Chromium fetching trick segments at 4x with no error, VP9), fast-ladder 56/56, test_fast_trick_play 37/37, test_fast_playout 31/31, fast suites, jobs, trickplay, trickplay-dash green.

## 2026-11-03 - I-frame trick-play that follows the playout splice, HLS (build queue 3.2b-7 (c); v0.39.0)

What: live channels and events with trick-play on and playout splicing on (input failover to the slate or loop) keep I-frame playlists in their HLS, and those playlists follow the splice. Before (3.2b-3) they were left out of the master for as long as playout could splice. The DASH trick set under a splice, rights variants and Christian's checks are split to 3.2b-8.

- **Playout** (`volt-encoder/bin/playout.py` `Track.render_iframes`, `Playout.render_iframes`): one entry per delivered entry of the variant (live segment or loop slot), the same media sequence, discontinuity sequence, discontinuities and EXT-X-MAP as its media playlist, each the byte range of the segment's opening keyframe in the same file. A segment whose range is not known yet starts the list after it, so the numbering never breaks.
- **Encoder** (`encoder.py` `_playout_pass`, `_keyframe_lookup`, `_keyframe_prune`, `_write_companion`): when the packager's master names I-frame playlists (the switch is on), each video variant gets `<label>.iframes_cc.m3u8`; ranges read once per segment from its first 16 KB with `fast_playout.keyframe_range` (the rule every Volt I-frame list uses) and forgotten when the segment leaves the window. `master_cc.m3u8` names the delivered playlists with the packager's attributes, leaves a line out until its playlist exists, and never names the packager's. Nothing encoded or stored beyond the playlist.
- **Rights variants**: unchanged and still safe: a variant master lists no I-frame playlist, and one asked for by name is refused by its content (`#EXT-X-I-FRAMES-ONLY`), whatever it is called.
- **Docs**: Account settings guide (what live gets under a slate or loop, what not yet). No console change, no screenshot.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `dev/test_splice_trick_play.py` 33/33 on the device (errors on HEAD): ffmpeg-made live and loop media through a real `Playout`, a 30 s outage and a 30 s forced loop, both variants listing exactly what their media playlists deliver after the window rolled, none of the 8 live segments consumed under the forced loop listed, 36/36 ranges decoding alone to one keyframe equal to the delivered segment's first picture, unknown-range numbering, master naming, off writes nothing. Encoder suites: test_trick_play 29/29, playout 64/64, companion-pdt 38/38, ladder-args 14/14, fairplay-drm 16/16, fast_trick_play 37/37, fast_playout 31/31, timed_events 65/65, id3_emsg 51/51. Container, Postgres 16: edge-variant-manifests 73/73 (one new case), edge-variants 62/62, edge-rights 38/38, rights-preview 50/50, trickplay-live 12/12, trickplay 54/54, trickplay-dash 66/66, trickplay-fast-dash 24/24, trickplay-dash-browser ok. Docs scrub and coverage clean.
- **Not run here**: Shaka Packager's live segments (not in the container or the device shell). Christian to force the loop on a channel with trick-play on and check Safari scrubbing over it (3.2b-8 (e)).
- **Not done**: 3.2b-8.


## 2026-11-04 - DASH trick-mode that follows the playout splice (build queue 3.2b-8 (c2); v0.40.0)

What: live channels and events with trick-play on, DASH packaged and playout splicing on (input failover to the slate or loop) now have a DASH trick-mode track while they can splice, and it follows the splice. Before, the spliced `manifest_cc.mpd` had no trick set in any Period. Rights variants and Christian's checks are split to 3.2b-9.

- **Live Periods** (`volt-encoder/bin/encoder.py` `_mpd_reps`, `_mpd_timeline`, `_mpd_trick`): the packager's keyframe-only set (3.2b-4, `<label>_trick/`) is read from its MPD and matched to the delivered video track it was cut from; segments the packager has named are remembered while their files are there, because after a break the delivered window reaches further back than the packager's own. `playout.py` `render_mpd(trick=)` / `_trick_set` lists them over exactly that track's media time in the Period (same presentationTimeOffset, consecutive numbers).
- **Loop Periods**: `encoder.py` `prepare_loop_trick` re-wraps each loop segment's opening keyframe once when the loop is prepared, for the rendition the packager cuts its trick track from (`filler/<label>/trick/k_<i>.m4s`, `mp4util.keyframe_fragment`, the Python twin of `services/trickplay/iframes.ts` `keyframeFragment`; clear tracks only, which the loop always is). `playout.py` `_loop_slots(trick=True)` re-times one per loop slot (`trick/p<run>_<k>.m4s` via `write_trick_slot`), the last cut to end with a closed Period, dropped when the Period leaves the window. Bitrate in the MPD: the loop's peak keyframe rate.
- **Every Period** gets its own trick AdaptationSet with the dashif trickmode property naming that Period's video set. A Period first published without one keeps none (an AdaptationSet must not appear in a Period after the fact). Switched off, nothing is listed or prepared.
- **Docs**: Account settings guide (live streams keep the DASH trick track under a slate or loop). No console change, no screenshot.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `dev/test_splice_trick_dash.py` 32/32 on the device (H.264) and in the container (VP9), errors on HEAD; new `dev/splice_trick_dash_shaka.mjs` 7/7 (Shaka in Chromium fast-forwarding at 4x from the live Period into the loop Period: 3 live trick segments and 3 loop keyframe slots fetched, all found, no error). Encoder suites: test_trick_play 29/29, test_splice_trick_play 33/33, playout 64/64, companion-pdt 38/38, ladder-args 14/14, fairplay-drm 16/16, vod-encoder 18/18, source-control 11/11, fast_trick_play 37/37, fast_playout 31/31, timed_events 65/65, id3_emsg 51/51, gfx_burn 54/54. Container, Postgres 16: edge-variant-manifests 73/73, edge-variants 62/62, rights-preview 50/50, live-scte35 43/43, encoder-task-disk 42/42, encoder-log-times 9/9, trickplay-live 12/12, trickplay 54/54, trickplay-dash 66/66, trickplay-fast-dash 24/24, trickplay-dash-browser ok. Docs scrub and coverage clean.
- **Not run here**: Shaka Packager's own live trick segments (stood in for by keyframe fragments of the live segments). A live trick segment can run past a closed Period's end by up to one segment (only the loop's last slot is cut); trick segments are only appended while fast-forwarding, not in normal play.
- **Not done**: 3.2b-9 (inside rights variants; Christian's checks on the Mac, now including forcing the loop and fast-forwarding over it in a DASH player).

## 2026-11-04 - Deploy and rollback script (build queue D1; v0.43.0)

What: one command deploys a release to the staging stack or an AWS environment, checks it serves, and puts the previous version back when it does not; another rolls back on request. What runs where is recorded.

- **Where**: `scripts/deploy/deploy.mjs` (the steps), `scripts/deploy/lib.mjs` (version resolution, the record, task definition retagging, the smoke test), `scripts/deploy/drivers/ecs.mjs` (aws CLI: ECR, register-task-definition from the newest revision Terraform wrote with only the image changed, a one-off Fargate migration task in the control-plane service's subnets, update-service, services-stable plus a check that the circuit breaker did not roll it back, per-stream families after the smoke test), `scripts/deploy/drivers/compose.mjs` (`VOLT_IMAGE_TAG` in `deploy/staging/docker-compose.yml`, `compose run` for the migrations, `up --wait`), `deploy/environments.json`, `release/deployments.json` (written by the script: running, prior, history). `volt-app/src/migrateOnly.ts` applies migrations and the boot top-ups (moved unchanged from `index.ts` to `services/bootSchema.ts`) under the boot lock and exits. Terraform: `control_plane_image_tag` required and validated (no latest), `encoder_image_tag` (default: the same) replaces the six `:latest` per-stream images, `image_tags` output, services `ignore_changes = [task_definition]` so an apply cannot undo a rollback. Staging compose fixed on the way: mailpit sat inside the app's `depends_on` (compose refused the file) and `IMAGE_SIGNING_SECRET` was not passed (the API would not start).
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `test/deploy-script.check.ts` 59/59 on the device: version resolution, the record, retagging, the smoke test against a local server, and the real script against an `aws` stand-in (dry run changes nothing; deploy; failing smoke test rolled back to the exact earlier revisions with the smoke re-run; circuit breaker refusal rolled back; failed migration stops before any service moves; explicit rollback; `--current`). **Real Docker staging stack in the container** (Postgres, Valkey, two api-public, scheduler, worker, nginx edge): first deploy (migrations 111 applied by the one-off container, then the services, smoke PASS ready and play gate), a second version deployed over it (0 migrations left), a version with a failing smoke test (real asset configured that does not exist) rolled back to the running one, `--rollback` back to the first, `--current`, and the record reading rollback, rolled-back, deployed, deployed. `terraform validate` and `fmt -check` clean, `terraform test` 10/10 (new `image_tags.tftest.hcl`: release tag everywhere, encoder tag apart, latest refused twice). terraform-env 57/57, chaos-harness 77/77, and in the container captions-settings 38/38, event-routes 29/29, fast-access 50/50 (boot with the moved schema top-ups).
- **Not done**: a real AWS deploy and rollback (Christian: add an `ecs` entry to `deploy/environments.json`, apply with a release tag, then `deploy.mjs <env> --dry-run` and a deploy). The image build itself was not run here (the container cannot reach npm from inside a build without its proxy certificate; the staging runs used an image built from the same sources); a staging run on the Mac builds it. The manifest step was skipped on staging (no asset that always plays): set `smoke.appKey` and `smoke.assetId`. D2 (migration safety check) is next; the script already runs `scripts/release/check-migrations.mjs` when it exists.

## 2026-11-04 - Migration safety check (build queue D2; v0.44.0)

What: a migration the previous release could not run on is refused before it is committed, released or deployed, so a rolling deploy (old and new tasks on one database) and a rollback (which keeps the schema) stay safe. Removing a column or table takes two releases and a declaration; concurrent indexes are now possible.

- **Where**: `scripts/release/migrations.mjs` (the rules: a lexical SQL reader, per-statement analysis, contract declarations, the baseline), `scripts/release/check-migrations.mjs` (the command: default, `--staged`, `--all`, `--update-baseline`), `scripts/release/check.mjs` (runs it, so the pre-commit hook and `deploy.mjs` step 1 do), `scripts/release/release.mjs` (refuses an unsafe migration before writing anything, then records the release's migrations as shipped and commits `release/migrations-baseline.json`), `scripts/deploy/deploy.mjs` (its separate optional call removed: check.mjs covers it), `volt-app/src/services/migrate.ts` (`-- volt:no-transaction` files run statement by statement, recorded only when all succeed; `splitSql`). Rule written into `RELEASING.md` ("Schema: add only"), `BUILD_RUNBOOK.md` and `docs/engineering/deploying-to-aws.md`.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `test/migration-safety.check.ts` 60/60 in the container (51/51 on the device without the database part): 12 passing and 21 failing sample migrations, the command on a scratch checkout (baseline, a bad new file with file and line, a contract that fixes it, the next release's baseline, an edited and a removed shipped file, a new boot top-up that drops), the real repo (passes with 111 shipped files baselined; `--all` finds 79 problems in 43 files, the 0027 and 0080 type changes among them), and the runner against Postgres (a no-transaction CONCURRENTLY index applied and valid, a failing statement named and not recorded, re-run fixed, the same index without the marker refused by Postgres). `deploy-script` 59/59. `check.mjs` and `check.mjs --staged` pass on the real tree.
- **Not done**: SQL built inside a string and run with EXECUTE is not seen. Whether the code really stopped using a removed column is declared, not proven. The 75 blocking indexes already shipped stay as they are (baselined).

## 2026-11-04 - Workflow report in the console (build queue W1; v0.45.0)

What: the Workflows page gets a Report tab built from the prototype dashboard, on real data. For 24 hours, 7 or 30 days, each stage on the map shows its runs, error rate, p50 and p95 time, retries and most common error code; clicking a stage gives succeeded, failed, cancelled, waiting time and the top five codes. Below: runs and error rate per lane per hour or day, add-on runs per add-on and hook, and background work no stage claims.

- **Where**: `volt-app/src/services/workflows/rollup.ts` (the rollups and the report), `services/workflows/map.ts` (`JOB_STAGES`, `REPORT_SKIPS_JOBS`), `services/jobs/errorCode.ts` (a code for every failure) and `services/jobs/worker.ts` (stores it), `services/jobs/owner.ts` (the job-to-account rule, moved unchanged from `modules/jobs.ts` so the rollup shares it), `modules/workflows.ts` (`GET /api/workflows/report`), `index.ts` (rollup started on the leader), migration `0126_workflow_report_indexes.sql` (`-- volt:no-transaction`, three concurrent indexes), `volt-web/src/pages/Workflows.tsx` and `workflows.css` (Report tab), `volt-web/src/api.ts`. Docs: `WORKFLOWS.md` ("The report"), the Workflows guide (Read the report, troubleshooting, API), screenshots `workflows` and new `workflows-report`, the docs seed (a week of finished work, fixed patterns), OpenAPI overlay entry.
- **How it counts**: jobs by type (a retry is one run with a retry; queued and running work not counted; `addon.dispatch` skipped because add-on runs come from `addon_runs`); Encode (live) from stream stops, a fault stop a failure (`stream_fault`); Record from completed recordings with their length; Review from decisions; add-on runs with timed out as a failure. Rollups: whole minutes recomputed and replaced each pass (five minutes rewind for late commits, one minute lag), so passes are idempotent; days rebuilt from their minutes; minutes kept 14 days.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `test/workflow-report.check.ts` 43/43 in the container (error code shapes; planted jobs, events and add-on runs; 31-pass backfill; recompute and second pass give identical rows; per stage counts, rates, retries, p50/p95 within the sketch's 1 %, codes; event-fed stages; other jobs; add-on runs; lane series per day and hour; 7 against 30 days; account isolation; 422 and 401; the worker storing a code on a plain thrown error). Around it: workflows 72/72, workflow-fixes 27/27, watchfolder-share-addon 58/58, jobs 43/43, fast-access 50/50, qoe-reports 71/71, qoe-reports-more 70/70, migration-safety 60/60 (the new no-transaction migration passes the D2 rule), docs-coverage 319/319; coverage and scrub checks clean. - **Not here**: `docs-seed-fast` 14/20 and `docs.check.sh` fail in the container with or without this change (the unchanged seed gives the same 14/20; `docs.check.sh` needs `volt-app/api/`, not copied).
- **Not done**: the rollup tables stay in the analytics Postgres when QoE moves to ClickHouse (a few thousand rows a day per account). Source, Package, Deliver and Ingest rules have nothing that records runs, so they show "No figures". The first start backfills 30 days, one day a pass (about half a minute).

## 2026-11-04 - Hybrid Sites 1: sites, hosts and enrolment (build queue H1; v0.46.0)

What: the first phase of the Hybrid Sites add-on (HYBRID_SITES.md, saved from Christian's design doc). A customer location is a site; a server there runs the Volt site agent, enrols with a one-time token, reports what it has, and waits for an administrator to approve it. Licensed per account and off by default: without the licence nothing new appears and every existing behaviour is unchanged.

- **Where**: migration `0127_hybrid_sites.sql` (`accounts.licences`, `sites`, `site_enrolment_tokens`, `site_hosts`, `site_credentials`; add only), `volt-app/src/services/sites/licences.ts` (the licence list and gate), `services/sites/sites.ts` (health, enrolment, credentials and rotation, heartbeat, config), `modules/sites.ts` (`/api/sites` for account administrators, `/api/site-agent` for the agent), `modules/platform.ts` (`licences` on `PATCH /api/platform/accounts/:id`), `auth/routes.ts` (`/auth/me` returns `account.licences`), `auth/roles.ts` (`/api/sites` under Account settings), `scripts/api-routes.ts` (the agent's credential named), `scripts/seed-docs.ts` (a licensed docs account with a site and two hosts). `volt-agent/src/site.ts` and `cli.ts` (0.2.0: `site enrol`, `site run`). `volt-web/src/pages/Sites.tsx`, `App.tsx` (Admin > Media setup > Sites, only for administrators of a licensed account), `pages/SystemAdmin.tsx` (Add-ons: a Hybrid Sites switch on the client account drawer), `api.ts`. Guide `docs-site/docs/guides/hybrid-sites.md`, coverage entry (batch H1, required), screenshot `sites`, API overlay and the `Sites` tag.
- **How it works**: tokens (`vsenrol_`) and credentials (`vsite_`) are kept as SHA-256 hashes; a token works once and expires (24 h by default). A pending host may heartbeat and pull config but is told `waiting_for_approval`; rejecting revokes its credentials. Heartbeat every 30 s, stale after 90 s, offline after 10 min; site health from approved hosts only. A change an agent should see bumps `sites.config_version` and the heartbeat says when to pull. Rotation hands a new credential over on the next heartbeat (again if missed) and the old one stops the first time the new one is used. A host credential is refused by `requireAuth`, so it reaches no console or admin route. Enrolment, decisions, tokens and rotation are audited.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web, volt-agent). New `test/sites.check.ts` 88/88 in the container: licence off (403 `licence_required`, `/auth/me`, nothing created), the system admin sets it (owner refused, unknown licence 422), sites (create, name clash, list, edit with versions, operator and other account refused), enrolment (shown once, hash only, spent once, expired and withdrawn refused), credential scope (six console and admin routes refused, no self-approval), pending then approved, stale and offline, a rejected second host, rotation (hand-over, missed hand-over, old credential dead after first use), disabled site, licence withdrawn (refused, nothing deleted), audit rows, and the real agent: facts read from the machine, enrol, state file mode 600, approval and rotation picked up, and `volt-agent site run` as a second process heartbeating against the control plane. Browser (Chromium, seeded docs account): 14/14 (create a site, enrolment token, enrol, approve, rotate, 1440 and 390 px, hidden for an operator and for an unlicensed account). docs-coverage 321/321 on the device; coverage and scrub clean; OpenAPI up to date. Full run in the container: 278/294 suites after re-runs; of the other 16, 15 fail the same way at HEAD in the container (asset-images, captions-gpu-sharing, deploy-script, docs-seed-fast, engineering-guides, fast.check.sh, live-degraded, mac-snapshot, metadata-events, review-qc, setup-images-ui, uploads, vod-library-pure, xmltv-import 34/35, xmltv-timezone) and docs-coverage fails there only because the screenshots were not copied (321/321 on the Mac). listStandard 32 endpoints, 3008 requests, all passed; terraform-env 58/58.
- **Not here**: the System Admin switch was not clicked in a browser (the platform console needs a TOTP sign-in); its route is covered by the suite. GPU facts come from `nvidia-smi` and were not seen on real NVIDIA hardware (this Mac and the container have none); encode sessions are reported only when `VOLT_NVENC_SESSIONS` is set.
- **Not done**: pools, watchfolders and work in the site config (H2 onwards); signed agent updates; creating worker VMs through a customer's hypervisor; a run on a second machine on another network.

## 2026-11-05 - Hybrid Sites 2: on-prem watchfolders, upload to the cloud (build queue H2; v0.47.0)

What: the first sellable piece of the Hybrid Sites add-on, for sites without encoders. A watchfolder can be on a customer's own storage at a site; the site agent sorts it with the folder's rules, keeps refused files at the site, and uploads accepted video to Volt, which encodes it like any upload.

- **Where**: migration `0128_site_watchfolders.sql` (kind check widened to `site`; `watchfolders.site_id`, `placement`; `watchfolder_items.upload_session_id`, `vod_asset_id`, `site_action`; add only). `volt-app/src/services/sites/siteWatchfolders.ts` (folders for a site, decisions, the cloud's second classification, pending uploads, returns, scan reports, the item an upload belongs to and closing it), `modules/siteTus.ts` (`/api/site-agent/tus`, the same tus server and upload sessions, opened only for an accepted item of the host's own site), `modules/tus.ts` (`landTus` shared, `PgTusStore` exported), `modules/sites.ts` (the agent's watchfolder routes), `services/sites/sites.ts` (the folders in the agent's config), `modules/watchfolders.ts` (kind `site` with licence, site and placement; `GET /site-options`; Scan now refused; Reprocess through the agent), `services/watchfolder/store.ts` (`toScanFolder` shared; the server's loader skips site folders), `services/watchfolder/scanner.ts` (an optional checksum hook), `server.ts`. `volt-agent/src/siteWatch.ts` (the pass), `site.ts` (0.3.0: the watch loop beside the heartbeat), `tsconfig.json` (rootDir the repo, so the agent compiles the cloud's scanner and rules from their own files; the CLI is now `dist/volt-agent/src/cli.js`). `volt-web/src/pages/Watchfolders.tsx`, `api.ts`. Guides `watchfolders.md` (Create an on-prem watchfolder, new screenshot `watchfolders-on-prem`), `hybrid-sites.md` (Watch a folder at the site); API overlay entry; HYBRID_SITES.md build status.
- **How it works**: the agent pulls its site's folders with their rules every pass (15 s, never two at once, beside the 30 s heartbeat), scans with the quiet-file rule (10 s for a site folder), moves a refused file into the review folder at the site and reports it (a failed report waits in an outbox in the state file), and reports an acceptance, which the cloud classifies again with its own copy of the rules (409 `rules_changed` if they moved in between; the agent decides again next pass). For placement `upload_cloud` it uploads each accepted video file; the upload session is opened for the item and kept on it, so a second create, from a restarted agent or one that lost its state file, answers with the same upload and the agent resumes from the cloud's offset. On the last byte the normal upload path runs with the folder as the source (ingest rules, duplicate check, metadata, VOD encode or Loops & Fillers); the asset is marked as a watchfolder asset and the item closes In library. A pending host, another site's host and another account are refused everywhere (403 `waiting_for_approval`, 404). Reprocess marks the item `site_action = 'return'`; the agent moves the file back, or the row returns to Needs review with why.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web, volt-agent). New `test/site-watchfolders.check.ts` 67/67 in the container (fails at HEAD, where the routes and the agent module do not exist): licence off, creating (no site, another account's site, no path, Encode at site refused, config version bumped), Scan now refused, credential scope (pending host, another site, another account, a console session, a key outside the folder), a pass over a real folder (two refusals moved to `_review` on site and listed with their reasons, a 2 s ffmpeg programme uploaded byte for byte and its VOD encode queued, an image target refused, the quiet-file rule, last scan time), rules changed, a 3 MB upload cut after 1 MB and finished by a fresh agent with no memory sending only the missing bytes (one session, file identical, later passes upload nothing), Reprocess round trip (decided again with a new rule; a file moved away returns to review with why), the server's scan and ingest passes leaving site folders alone, and `volt-agent site run` as a second process taking a new drop into the library. Watchfolder suites 8/8 (395 assertions), sites, tus 21/21, uploads 49/49, upload-staging 42/42, upload-retry 20/20, ingest-core 97/97, migration-safety 60/60, docs-coverage 323/323, listStandard ok, terraform-env 58/58 on the Mac, migration safety ok, coverage and scrub clean. Browser (Chromium, real API and console): 19/19 at 1440 and 390 px (the site named on the list and page, the New watchfolder form, Where it is encoded, no Scan now, the refused file at the site in the queue, Sent back waiting for the agent after Reprocess, no page errors). Full run in the container: 269/295; the other 26 fail the same way at HEAD in the same copy (52 failed assertions both: asset-images, captions-gpu-sharing, deploy-script, docs-seed-fast, docs.check.sh, e2e-runsheet, engineering-guides, fast-ladder, fast.check.sh, images, live-degraded, live-scte35, mac-snapshot, metadata-events, packager-heartbeat, radio-id3, radio-songs, reframe, review-qc, setup-images-ui, terraform-env (needs BUILD_QUEUE.md beside the copy; 58/58 on the Mac), trickplay-fast-dash, trickplay-variants-dash, vod-library-pure, vod-library, xmltv-import).
- **Not here**: the encode itself ran only as the queued job (no Shaka Packager in the container). The Mac run with a folder standing for customer storage is Christian's: start the API, make a site with an approved host and an on-prem folder on a local path, run `npx tsx ../volt-agent/src/cli.ts site run <state.json>`, drop a programme and a `.prproj`, and check the programme plays once encoded and the project file is in `_review` and on the folder page.
- **Not done**: images from a site (an image target is refused with the reason); Encode at site (H4); fallback to the cloud after a site goes offline (H5); the Watchfolders page at phone width (WF-phone, an overflow every folder already had).

## 2026-11-05 - Watchfolder default rules by file type (build queue H2b, first part; v0.48.0)

What: a new watchfolder takes images and video in from the first drop instead of sending everything to review until someone writes rules, and a rule can no longer send a type of file somewhere that cannot take it. H2b was more than one run's work, so it was split: this run built (a) for images and video and the type check of (d); H2b-2 (companions by name), H2b-3 (slates to Loops & Fillers, image types from the name or the rule) and H2b-4 (audio assets; images, audio and companions from a site) follow.

- **Where**: migration `0129_watchfolder_default_rules.sql` (`watchfolder_rules.builtin`, nullable; add only). New `volt-app/src/services/watchfolder/defaults.ts` (file types and their extensions, `typesOfPattern`, the default rules per folder kind, `customOrdinal` and `defaultOrdinals`). `services/watchfolder/rules.ts` (`extension` rules take a list; a single extension behaves as before; split inline because the site agent compiles this file on its own). `modules/watchfolders.ts` (defaults on create unless `defaultRules: false`, returned as `rules`; `POST /:id/rules/defaults`, audited; `defaultsMissing` on the folder; `targetKinds` with `accepts`; custom rules placed above the defaults; `assertFitsType` on create and on edit of pattern, match type, kind or action). `volt-web/src/pages/Watchfolders.tsx` and `api.ts` (Default tag, On and Off per rule, Add default rules, destinations filtered by the pattern's file type with a line saying why). `scripts/seed-docs.ts` (the docs folder shows one rule of its own above the two defaults). Guide `watchfolders.md` (Default rules by file type, the type check, troubleshooting, API), screenshot `watchfolders` retaken; API overlay entry for the new route; routes and OpenAPI regenerated.
- **How it works**: defaults start at ordinal 100000 (`DEFAULTS_FROM`), so a rule an operator adds later lands after their other rules and before the defaults and wins over them (a full gap halves; no gap at all appends at the end). A site folder gets the video rule only, because the agent carries only video across until H2b-4. "Add default rules" adds only the defaults a folder lacks, after everything it has, so a folder made before this release does exactly what it did for any file an existing rule catches. The type check reads the extensions a pattern can only match (`extension` lists, a glob or suffix ending in a known extension); a pattern that could match anything (`loop_*`, a regex) or an unknown extension (`.pdf`) is not checked, and refusing rules are never checked.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `test/watchfolder-defaults.check.ts` 81/81 in the container (fails at HEAD: the module and the route are new): the pure parts (extension lists, typesOfPattern, ordinals, defaults per kind), a new folder's rules and order, the dry run (png, JPEG, mp4, mxf in; prproj, mp3, vtt, a .part to review), a real scan of a fresh folder (a 64x36 ffmpeg png into the Image Library, a 1 s H.264 mp4 into the Media Library with its encode queued, a project file moved to `_review`), custom rules above the defaults and winning, five refusals and three allowed shapes of the type check (also on edit), Off and On, delete and Add default rules (201 then 200 with nothing added, 404 for another account, audited), an older folder keeping its own rule first, `defaultRules: false`, a bucket folder. Watchfolder suites 9/9 (476 assertions), site-watchfolders ok (its folder now created with `defaultRules: false`: its rules-changed case needs a .mov that no rule takes; nothing else in it changed), sites ok, tus 21/21, uploads 49/49, upload-staging 42/42, upload-retry 20/20, ingest-core 97/97, server-status 10/10, migration-safety 60/60; docs-coverage 323/323 on the Mac. Browser (Chromium, real API on the docs seed, console from vite): 14/14 at 1440 px; looked at the 1440 and 390 px screenshots.
- **Not here**: the full suite run was not repeated this time (the change is contained in the watchfolder module, and every suite that touches it ran). At 390 px the rule rows overflow, as they already did (WF-phone).
- **Not done**: companions by name (H2b-2); slates to Loops & Fillers and image types (H2b-3); audio assets, and images, audio and companions from a site, with the image default on site folders (H2b-4). Site agents must be built from this release or later to read extension lists (an older agent sends such a file to review at the site, where Reprocess recovers it).

## 2026-11-05 - Watchfolder companions by name (build queue H2b-2; v0.49.0)

What: a caption or metadata file dropped into a watchfolder is attached to the video it is named after, the way subtitling houses name them: `Scrubs-s1-e1_en.vtt` gives `Scrubs-s1-e1.mp4` an English subtitle track in its HLS and DASH. Until now such files went to review.

- **Where**: new `volt-app/src/services/watchfolder/companionIngest.ts` (the `companion` target kind, `prefixFits` and `bestMatch`, the pass with its store contract); `services/watchfolder/store.ts` (`pendingCompanionItems`, `pgCompanionStore`, the pass run last in `ingestFolder` so a caption dropped beside its video attaches in the same scan; review files of a bucket folder mirrored back); `services/watchfolder/defaults.ts` (two more default rules, Captions to their video `srt, vtt, ttml, dfxp` and Metadata files to their video `json, xml, nfo, csv`, both `companion`, builtin `captions` and `sidecars`; site folders still video only); `services/watchfolder/videoIngest.ts` (`markLanded` now keeps the asset on the item, `watchfolder_items.vod_asset_id`, so a later caption finds its video); `modules/watchfolders.ts` (`targetKinds()` offers `companion`, goesTo `companions`, accepts caption and sidecar, so the type check of H2b (d) allows only those files there); `services/sites/siteWatchfolders.ts` (the refusal message names companions); `volt-web/src/pages/Watchfolders.tsx` (an "Attach to its video" group in the rule form; Waiting's help says a companion waits up to 24 h). No migration: the column it uses came with 0128.
- **How it works**: the companion's name without its extension must start with the video's (case ignored) and continue with `_`, `.`, `-`, a space or nothing, so `Show-e1` never takes `Show-e10_en.vtt`; the longest such name wins, then the same subfolder, then a landed video over a waiting one, then the newest. Candidates are only this folder's videos: landed (`status ingested` with its asset still there) or accepted and still waiting, and a waiting video that fits best makes the caption wait for it. Attaching goes through `services/companions/store.ts addFile` (source `watchfolder`), so language, sdh and forced come from `nameHints` and the caption check, WebVTT and track delivery are those of an upload; a caption that fails its check is attached switched off and the item says so; a sidecar is attached for a person to apply (`proposeMetadata`), never applied. Over 25 MB (the store's limit) goes to Needs review at once; no video after 24 h (from `seen_at`) goes to Needs review with the file moved to the review folder; until then the item is Waiting with "waiting for its video". Filename metadata as today (`ingestLanded`): `Scrubs-s1-e1.mp4` is titled "Episode 1 (S1)" with series Scrubs, season 1, episode 1.
- **Checked**: watchfolder-companions 67/67 (new; fails at HEAD: the module is new), watchfolder-defaults 81/81 (updated for the four defaults), the other watchfolder suites 9/9 (watchfolder-api 26, -ingest 70, -rules 43, -s3-events 50, -scan 33, -share-addon 58, -video-landing 45, -video 70), site-watchfolders ok, sites ok, companion-files 64/64, ingest-core 97/97, uploads 49/49, upload-staging 42/42, upload-retry 20/20, tus 21/21, server-status 10/10 in the container; docs-coverage 323/323 and migration-safety 51/51 on the Mac; typechecks clean (volt-app, volt-web); browser 9/9. The suite runs real folders through the real API: a video then its caption (title and series, season, episode from the name; one English default track; the track in a real HLS master and DASH MPD through `withTracks`; listed by `GET /api/vod/{id}/files`), the caption first (Waiting, then attached in the pass the video lands), `Show-e1` and `Show-e10` with their captions in one scan, sdh and forced (FORCED=YES in HLS), an unreadable srt (attached off), a JSON sidecar (attached, nothing applied), a 26 MB caption (review at once, moved), a caption with no video moved past 24 h (review, moved, in the review queue), and a caption in another folder (not matched). Browser against the real API and console: the four default rules, a `*_subs.vtt` pattern offers only "Attach to its video", `*.png` does not, In library shows "attached to "Episode 1 (S1)" as English subtitles", Waiting shows "waiting for its video", the asset's Companion files tab lists the track as English, no page errors. Guide `watchfolders.md` updated (default rules table, a "Captions and metadata files by name" section, statuses, troubleshooting, API); screenshot `watchfolders` retaken from the docs seed.
- **Not here**: the full suite run was not repeated (the change is contained in the watchfolder pass, and every suite that touches it ran). Videos taken in by a local folder before this release have no asset on their item, so a caption for one of them waits and then goes to review; add it on the asset page, or drop the video again.
- **Not done**: slates to Loops & Fillers and image types (H2b-3); audio assets, and images, audio and companions from a site (H2b-4). At 390 px the rule rows still overflow (WF-phone).

## 2026-11-05 - Watchfolder slates to Loops & Fillers, and image types from the name or the rule (build queue H2b-3; v0.50.0)

What: bulk artwork finds its episode. `Scrubs-s1-e1-thumbnail.jpg` and `Scrubs-s1-e1_poster.png` dropped beside `Scrubs-s1-e1.mp4` become its Thumbnail and Poster, as if chosen on the asset page; a folder whose names carry no type word sets the type on the rule. A rule can also send an image straight into Loops & Fillers as a slate.

- **Where**: new `volt-app/src/services/watchfolder/imageTypes.ts` (pure: `typeWordOf`, `resolveImageType`, the word table), `slateIngest.ts` (target `filler-slate`), `imageAttach.ts` (the attach pass and its store contract); `ingest.ts` (the image pass reads the type and files an `image` target by it; `markIngested` takes the wanted type); `store.ts` (`pendingItems` joins the rule's type, the slate pass, the attach pass last in `ingestFolder`, `videoImageTypes`, `pgSlateStore`, `pgImageAttachStore`); `modules/watchfolders.ts` (`filler-slate` in `targetKinds`, `imageType` and `imageTypeFromName` on rule create and edit, `imageTypes` on the folder, the dry run's `imageType`, `libraryKind`, `asset`); `rules.ts` (`WatchRule.imageType`); migration `0130_watchfolder_image_types.sql` (add only: rule `image_type`, `image_type_from_name` default true; item `image_type`, `image_attach`); `volt-web` `api.ts` and `pages/Watchfolders.tsx` (Image type picker and "A type word in the name wins" for Image Library rules, the type on the rule row, the type and asset in Try a filename); `scripts/seed-docs.ts`.
- **How it works**: the word is read from the end of the name after `-`, `_`, `.` or a space, longest first (so `logo-title` is title art, not a brand logo), against the account's enabled video types: the standard words, then a type's key or label with separators ignored. The image goes into the Image Library at once, then is matched to its video like a caption (`companionIngest.bestMatch`) and set with `assetImageSet.setTypeImage`; it waits up to 24 hours for a video, then is marked unmatched and stays in the library. Fit warnings (`fitWarnings`) are written on the item. A slate is written to the filler store before its row says ready, as a copy from the library is.
- **Checked**: watchfolder-image-types 69/69 (new; fails at HEAD: the modules are new): the word table, account types by key and label, switched-off types, rule type and the name switch; `filler-slate` offered for images only, a png lands a ready 1920x1080 slate with its bytes and an mp4 lands a loop; image type refused on a video rule and when unknown; thumbnail and poster after the video (poster_key set and locked, poster row, keyframe and poster kinds, "Not 2:3" warned and shown on the asset page); poster before its video; hero unmatched after 24 h (time moved) and still in the library; `Station-logo.png` a brand image; `*_key_art.jpg` with `imageType: hero`; the name switched off; the dry run. Watchfolder suites 11/11, site-watchfolders, sites, companion-files 64, ingest-core 97, uploads 49, upload-staging 42, upload-retry 20, tus 21, filler-library 33, filler-prep 15, filler-store 36, filler-thumbs 36, asset-image-types 51, server-status 10 in the container; migration-safety 51/51 on the Mac; typechecks clean; browser 13/13 (rule rows show the type, a png pattern offers `filler-slate` under Loops & Fillers, the picker only for Image Library targets, a rule added with a type and the name off, the dry run's type, rule type and no-asset wording, no page errors). Screenshots `watchfolders` and `watchfolders-on-prem` retaken from the docs seed.
- **Not here**: asset-images fails 8 of 114 in the container, the same 8 at HEAD (a song cover read from a web page), unrelated. The full suite run was not repeated.

## 2026-11-05 - Audio assets, and the audio default rule for watchfolders (build queue H2b-4, first part; v0.51.0)

What: an MP3, WAV, M4A, AAC or FLAC file uploaded or dropped into a watchfolder becomes an audio asset in the Media Library, encoded to its sound and played with the audio player. Before this the encode refused any file with no video track, so audio uploads failed and audio drops went to review. H2b-4 was split: this run built the audio assets; images, audio and companions from a site are H2b-5.

- **Where**: `volt-app/src/services/jobs/handlers/vodEncode.ts` (the sound-only path: no ladder fit, `audio_only` in the job, `kind: 'audio'` and `probe.hasVideo` in `encode_result`, no MP4, the picture steps skipped, `no_media` for a file with neither); `volt-encoder/bin/vod_encoder.py` (`audio_only`: no rungs, only the audio output, refused when the file has no sound either); `services/ingest/metadata.ts` (`interpretProbe` keeps `hasVideo` and `hasAudio`, so the encode's refresh of `ingest.probe` no longer drops them); `modules/vod.ts` (`audioOnly` on the asset also from the probe); `services/watchfolder/defaults.ts` (the `audio` default rule); `modules/watchfolders.ts` (Media Library targets accept audio, Loops & Fillers video only). No migration.
- **How it works**: the probe already ignored cover art (an mp3's `attached_pic`), so an mp3 with artwork is sound only. The ladder still gives the segment length and the packaging, so an audio asset cuts and packages like a video; its single AAC rendition is the encoder contract every encode's sound uses (`ladder_args.py audio_track_args`, 128 kb/s stereo 48 kHz). The library's existing audio test (`ingest.probe.hasVideo = false`) now holds for encoded files, so the grid shows the audio colour and square artwork, the asset page the audio player and the Encode tab "HLS (audio only)" and "DASH (audio only)" with no MP4. Uploads take the same encode, so they benefit too.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean on the device (volt-app, volt-web). New `test/vod-audio-asset.check.ts` 30/30 in the container (fails at HEAD: the probe has no `hasVideo` and the encode refuses the mp3): both probes on an mp3 with cover art; the encode through the real handler, ffmpeg and `vod_encoder.py` (job `audio_only`, no rungs, ready, HLS and DASH and no MP4, one AAC track read back from the packaged init segment at 48 kHz stereo, only an audio rendition on disk, the packager given one audio stream and both manifests, QC queued and nothing that needs a picture); the API's `audioOnly`, the list's `audio_only` and `media_kind`, the Encode tab's deliveries; an mp4 still a video; a subtitles-only file refused `no_media`; an mp3 dropped into a fresh folder taken into the Media Library with its encode queued; audio rules to Loops & Fillers and the Image Library refused, to the Media Library accepted. Watchfolder suites 12/12 (613 assertions; defaults 82/82 and companions 67/67 updated for the fifth default), site-watchfolders ok, ingest-core 97/97, vod-library 70/70, vod-source-store 30/30, fast-ladder 56/56, uploads 49/49, upload-staging 42/42, upload-retry 20/20, companion-files 64/64, asset-readiness 25/25, migration-safety 60/60, docs-coverage 323/323.
- **Not here**: Shaka Packager is not in the container, so the packaging step ran against a stub that records its arguments and writes what Shaka writes; the radio stations already package audio-only HLS and DASH with the real packager. Christian's check on the Mac: `PACKAGER_BIN=../packager-osx-arm64/packager npx tsx test/vod-audio-asset.check.ts` (it then reads the real master and MPD back), and drop an mp3 into a local watchfolder and play it on its asset page. No browser check: the container's Chromium plays no AAC. vod-library-pure fails 1 of 170 and asset-images 8 of 114, the same at HEAD. The watchfolders screenshot still shows four default rules; retake it with the screenshot script. The full suite run was not repeated.
- **Not done**: images, audio and companions from a site, and the default rules on site folders (H2b-5). The audio ladder is one AAC rendition (as radio); a second, lower rendition is a decision for Christian. At 390 px the rule rows still overflow (WF-phone).

## 2026-11-05 - Images, audio and companions from a Hybrid Site (build queue H2b-5; v0.52.0)

What: an on-prem watchfolder at a site now takes in every type a local folder does. Before this only video crossed from a site; an image was marked Failed and captions were not carried. The site agent uploads each accepted file on the same resumable tus path, and the cloud takes it in with the same passes as a local drop.

- **Where**: `volt-app/src/services/sites/siteWatchfolders.ts` (`siteTargetFor`, the ceilings checked at the decision, `landSiteFile`, `ingestSiteFolder`, `sendToReviewAtSite`, `pendingReviews` and `reviewed`; `pendingUploads` now lists every target and leaves out a landed file); `modules/siteTus.ts` (an image, slate or companion opens a `site_file` upload with no asset, and lands through `landSiteFile`); `modules/sites.ts` (`GET/POST /api/site-agent/watchfolders/:id/reviews`, and `/scanned` runs the folder's ingest); `services/watchfolder/store.ts` (`siteStagingRoot`, `loadIngestFolder(id, site)`, `pendingItems`/`pendingCompanionItems` with `landedOnly`); `services/watchfolder/defaults.ts` (site folders get all five defaults); `config.ts` (`SITE_STAGING_DIR`); migration `0131_site_review_action.sql` (`site_action` may be `review`; widened CHECK, NOT VALID then VALIDATE); `volt-agent/src/siteWatch.ts` (step 5, the review moves; tolerates a cloud without the list) and agent 0.4.0; `volt-web` Watchfolders (the placement help, "Waiting for the site agent to move it into the review folder").
- **How it works**: audio is a Media Library target, so it crosses as video does (uploaded as `audio/*`, then an audio asset, H2b-4). An image, slate or companion upload checks the SHA-256 of what arrived against the accepted file (changed on the way: Failed, the new content is a new item), puts the copy in `<SITE_STAGING_DIR>/<folder>/<key>` and runs `ingestSiteFolder`: the Image Library pass, slates to Loops & Fillers, companions to their video, typed images to their asset, over landed files only. The same ingest runs at the end of every agent pass, so a caption that waits for its video attaches in the pass the video lands in, and goes to review after 24 hours, with no scheduler of its own. A landed copy that is not on this server (another task on AWS, a replaced disk) is asked for again: the upload comes off the item and the agent sends it next pass. A companion sent to review is closed here as Needs review with its review key and `site_action = 'review'`; the agent moves the file into the review folder at the site and reports it, or the row says it could not. Over its ceiling (images the folder's `max_ingest_bytes`, slates 64 MB, companions 25 MB) a file is not uploaded at all: Failed, or review at the site.
- **Checked**: typechecks clean (volt-app and volt-web on the device; volt-agent in the container, the device has no node_modules for it). New `test/site-watchfolder-files.check.ts` 30/30 in the container with real ffmpeg media and the agent's own watch code (fails at HEAD: the folder gets the video rule only and the image is refused): image to the Image Library with its copy removed, slate to Loops & Fillers, mp3 to the Media Library as audio with its encode queued and byte for byte the original, caption attached to its video, a caption that waits, is not uploaded again and attaches when its video lands, a lost copy uploaded once more, 24 hours to review moved at the site, another site's host refused the list, a 26 MB caption to review and an image over the folder ceiling Failed with nothing uploaded, mangled bytes refused, Add default rules on an older site folder. Updated `site-watchfolders.check.ts` 67/67 (the png is now In library), `watchfolder-defaults` 82/82, `watchfolder-companions` 67/67. Around it: watchfolder-api 26, -image-types 69, -ingest 70, -rules 43, -s3-events 50, -scan 33, -share-addon 58, -video-landing 45, -video 70, sites ok, tus 21, uploads 49, upload-staging 42, upload-retry 20, ingest-core 97, companion-files 64, vod-audio-asset 30, filler-library 33, filler-store 36, server-status 10 (24/24 suites, 1015 assertions); migration-safety 51/51, check-migrations ok and docs-coverage 323/323 on the Mac. Browser at 1440 px: the folder page shows the review wait line and the new placement help; the `watchfolders-on-prem` screenshot retaken.
